Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The elements operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 197. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 265 with 5 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to elements theory, we decompose the problem into 197 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 265. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(5/n). QED.

Proof: Consider the isomorphism class operator L on the elements space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 667 for all k. By the isomorphism class theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 267. QED.
